There are no other white pieces on the board.

Therefore, g3g6 is checkmate.

Let's check other moves just in case.
g3h4+
King h6 could move to g6 (if not guarded) or h5 or g7.
If Qh4:
- King at h6 is attacked.
- King can move to g6? Kf6 guards g6.
- King can move to g7? Kf6 guards g7.
- King can move to h5? Qh4 guards h5.
- King can move to h7? Oh, the White King could potentially move to h7? No, Qh4 doesn't guard h7.
So Qh4+ allows Kh7.

Comparing Qg6+ and Qh4+:
Qg6 seems to be an immediate mate.

Let's re-verify the coordinate g3g6.
Queen at g3 moves to g6.
